\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{i=1,\atop i\neq 2^{m-k-1}}^{2^{m}-1}(\alpha^{(k)}_{n_{2i},n_{-1}}+\alpha^{(k)}_{n_{2i+1},n_{-1}})^{2}+(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{n_{-1},n_{-1}}-\alpha^{(k)}_{n_{s(n_{-1})},n_{-1}})^{2}+(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,n_{-1}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j\in[S]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{n_{-1}=j\right\}\left(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{l\in[S]\atop l\notin\{n_{1},j,s(j)\}}(\alpha^{(k)}_{l,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(l),j})^{2}+(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j})^{2}+(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j})^{2}\right)\right],

which gives (
49a
),
where the second equality follows from our assumption that
\{a_{i}\}_{i\in[S]}
are orthonormal, and in the third equality we use the fact that
n_{2^{m-k-1}}
is the parent of
n_{-1}
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\Delta_{y}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\alpha_{n_{i},n_{-1}}^{(k)}a_{n_{i}}-a_{n_{-1}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1,\atop i\neq 2^{m-k}}^{N}(\alpha_{n_{i},n_{-1}}^{(k)})^{2}+(1-\alpha_{n_{-1},n_{-1}}^{(k)})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j\in[S]}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{-1}=j\}\left(\sum_{l\in[S],\atop l\neq j}(\alpha_{l,j}^{(k)})^{2}+(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)})^{2}\right)\right].

B.1.3
Proof of Lemma
3

For notation simplicity, we let
n_{-1}=n_{-1}^{(k)}
.

Computation of
\delta x^{(k)}
.

The fact that
\delta x_{0,j}^{(k)}=0
follows from our assumption that
a_{0}=0
. Below we consider the case when
j\in[S]
.
By the chain rule, we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial H_{i,j}}=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=0}^{S}\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}\frac{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}{\partial H_{i,j}}\right].

(78)

We compute the terms in the summand separately.

•

When
p=j
, by (
49a
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(79)

•

When
p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}
, by (
49a
), we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbbm{1}\left\{n_{-1}=j,s(j)=p\right\}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{l\in[S]\atop l\notin\{n_{1},j,i\}}(\alpha^{(k)}_{l,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(l),j})^{2}+(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j})^{2}+(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j})^{2}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbbm{1}\left\{n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq p,n_{1}\neq p\right\}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{l\in[S]\atop l\notin\{n_{1},j,s(j)\}}(\alpha^{(k)}_{l,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(l),j})^{2}+(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j})^{2}+(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j})^{2}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbbm{1}\left\{n_{-1}=j,n_{1}=p\right\}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{l\in[S]\atop l\notin\{p,j,s(j)\}}(\alpha^{(k)}_{l,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(l),j})^{2}+(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j})^{2}+(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j})^{2}\Big)\Bigg],

from which we can compute that when
p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=p\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq p,n_{1}\neq p\right].

(80)

•

When
p=0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{0,j}^{(k)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(81)

In addition, by (
48
), we have for all
i,j\in\{0,1,\ldots,S\}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}}{\partial B}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}a_{i}a_{j}^{\top}-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\sum_{l=0}^{S}\alpha_{l,j}^{(k)}a_{l}a_{j}^{\top},

(82)

which gives

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}}{\partial H_{p,q}}=\begin{cases}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(1-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\right),\quad\text{when }p=i,q=j,\\
-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)},\quad\text{when }p\in[S]\setminus\{i\},q=j,\\
0,\quad\text{otherwise.}\end{cases}

(83)

Plugging (
83
) into (
78
), we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(k)}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{p=0\atop p\neq i}^{S}\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}\left(-\ \alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)+\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(1-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\right)\Bigg].

(84)

By (
84
), (
79
), (
•
‣
B.1.3
) and (
81
), we can compute that

\displaystyle\delta x_{j,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=p\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq p,n_{1}\neq p\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\left(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(85)

Note that the second term can be simplified as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=p\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],

(86)

and we can rewrite the third term in (
B.1.3
) as

\displaystyle-\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq p,n_{1}\neq p\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\notin\{p,s(p)\},n_{1}\notin\{p,s(p)\}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\notin\{p,s(p)\},n_{1}\notin\{p,s(p)\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{s(p),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\notin\{p,s(p)\},n_{1}\notin\{p,s(p)\}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\notin\{p,s(p)\},n_{1}\notin\{p,s(p)\}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j,p\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\bigg]

\displaystyle=-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\bigg],

(87)

where the second equality follows from the symmetry of the tree structure (a node
p
and its sibling
s(p)
are symmetric).
Thus we can rewrite (
B.1.3
) as

\displaystyle\delta x_{j,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],

(88)

which gives (
50a
).

Similarly, for
i\in[S]\setminus\{j\}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{i,j\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=p\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{i,j\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq p,n_{1}\neq p\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(1-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(i),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(1-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq i,n_{1}\neq i\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=p\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq p,n_{1}\neq p\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(i),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq i,n_{1}\neq i\right].

(89)

Similar as in (
B.1.3
), we can simplify the third term in (
B.1.3
) as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=p\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],

(90)

and similar to (
B.1.3
), we can rewrite the fourth term in (
B.1.3
) as

\displaystyle-\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq p,n_{1}\neq p\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\bigg]

(91)

Substituting (
B.1.3
) and (
B.1.3
) into (
B.1.3
) and reorganizing the terms, we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}+\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(i),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\right].

(92)

This gives (
50b
).

Computation of
\delta y^{(k)}
.

The fact that
\delta y_{0,j}^{(k)}=0
follows from our assumption that
a_{0}=0
, below we consider the case when
j\in[S]
.
By the chain rule, we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{i,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{\partial\Delta_{y}^{(k)}}{\partial H_{i,j}}=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=0}^{S}\frac{\partial\Delta_{y}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}\frac{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}{\partial H_{i,j}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:dalphadH}}{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=0\atop p\neq i}^{S}\frac{\partial\Delta_{y}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}\left(-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)+\frac{\partial\Delta_{y}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(1-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\right)\right].

(93)

By (
49b
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Delta_{y}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}|n_{-1}=j\right],&\text{if }p=j,\\
\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}|n_{-1}=j\right],&\text{if }p\in[S]\setminus\{j\},\\
0,&\text{if }p=0.\end{cases}

(94)

Plugging (
94
) into (
B.1.3
), we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{j,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\bigg(\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)^{2}+\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\bigg)\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right],

(95)

which gives (
51a
).

For
i\in[S]\setminus\{j\}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{i,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(1-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\right)+\left(\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\right)^{2}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)})\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg(\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)^{2}-\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg)\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right],

(96)

which gives (
51b
).

B.1.4
Proof of Lemma
4

For notation simplicity, we let
n_{-1}=n_{-1}^{(k)}
.
By (
50b
), for all
j\in[S]
and
i\in[S]\setminus\{j\}
,

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(k)}+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}+\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(i),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\underbrace{\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}+\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]}_{(a)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{S-2}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq i\right]

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{+\frac{S-2}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq i\right]}_{(b)}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]}_{(c)}.

(97)

Note that (a) and (c) add up to the following:

\displaystyle(a)+(c)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}+\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(98)

We could also rewrite (b) as follows:

\displaystyle(b)

\displaystyle=\frac{S-2}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\neq s(j)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j,s(j)\}}\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\frac{S-1}{N-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\mathbbm{1}\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\}\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\geq\underbrace{\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]}_{=-(a)-(c)\,\,\text{by \eqref{eq:a_plus_c}}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],

(99)

where the second line follows from the symmetry of the training distribution, and the last inequality uses the fact that
\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\geq\mathbbm{1}\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\}\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}
. This is because by symmetry we have
H_{s(j),j}=H_{i,j}
, and
N_{s(j)}^{(k-1)}=1
because
s(j)
is not on the path.

Plugging (
B.1.4
) and (
B.1.4
) into (
B.1.4
), we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(k)}+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{S-2}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)\neq i\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{S-3}{2S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\bigg]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]}_{(d)}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,s(j)=i\right]}_{(e)}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\frac{1}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]}_{(f)}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\frac{1}{2S}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{p\in[S]\atop p\notin\{j,s(j),n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\alpha^{(k)}_{s(p),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\Bigg]}_{(g)}.

(100)

Following the same logic as in the proof of (
B.1.4
), we have that
(e) can be expressed as

\displaystyle(e)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\mathbbm{1}\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\}\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(101)

Thus we have

\displaystyle(d)+(e)\geq\frac{N-2}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(102)

Furthermore, (g) can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle(g)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],

(103)

which gives

\displaystyle(f)+(g)\geq-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\frac{1}{N-1}\alpha_{0,j}^{(k)}\right)\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(104)

Substituting (
102
) and (
104
) into (
B.1.4
), we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(k)}+\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{N-2}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}+\frac{1}{N-1}\alpha_{0,j}^{(k)}\right)\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\frac{N-2}{N-1}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}-\max_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}-\frac{1}{N-1}\alpha_{0,j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(105)

By (
51b
) we have that

\displaystyle\delta y_{i,j}^{(k)}-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg(\sum_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)^{2}-\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}-\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg)\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{0,j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{(k)}_{i,j}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\bigg(\sum_{0\leq p\leq S\atop p\neq j}\left(\alpha_{p,j}^{(k)}\right)^{2}-\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\bigg)\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}-\max_{0\leq p\leq S\atop p\neq j}\alpha^{(k)}_{p,j}\right)\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right].

(106)

We have (
4
) follows from adding (
B.1.4
) and (
B.1.4
).

B.1.5
Proof of Lemma
5

Recall in Assumption
3
we require
m\geq 3
, which implies
N\geq 15
and (
57
) in turn implies

H_{j,j}^{(t)}\geq H_{i,j}^{(t)}
.
In addition, when
n_{-1}^{(k)}=j
, we have

N_{i,j}^{(k-1)}\leq 2
, and
N_{j,j}^{(k-1)}=1
.
Thus the above facts together with the definition of
\alpha
(c.f. (
48
)) lead to:

\displaystyle\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\geq\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{i,j},\quad\forall 0\leq i\leq S,i\neq j,

(107)

and

\displaystyle\forall t\in{\mathbb{N}},p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}:\alpha^{(t,k)}_{p,j}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\exp(H_{p,j}^{(t)})}{\sum_{q\in[S]}N_{q}^{(t,k-1)}\exp(H_{q,j}^{(t)})+1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\exp(H_{p,j}^{(t)})}{\exp(H_{j,j}^{(t)})+(N-1)\exp(H_{p,j}^{(t)})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\exp(H_{j,j}^{(t)}-H_{p,j}^{(t)})+N-1}\overset{\eqref{eq:IH1}}{\leq}\frac{2}{N},

(108)

where the second line follows from the symmetry of our training distribution (i.e.,
H_{i,j}
’s are equal for any
i\in[S]\setminus\{j\}
).
Thus by our choice of
T_{1}
(see (
54
)) and the fact that
\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\leq\alpha^{(t,1)}_{j,j}
for any
k\in[m]
, we have when
n_{-1}=j
:

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1}]:1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\geq\frac{1}{2},\quad\text{and}\quad 1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\geq\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{N}>\frac{1}{3},

(109)

and

\displaystyle\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\max_{0\leq p\leq S\atop p\neq j}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{p,j}\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq_alpha_jj}}{\geq}-\frac{1}{2}\max_{0\leq p\leq S\atop p\neq j}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{p,j}\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq_alpha_ij}}{\geq}-\frac{1}{N}.

(110)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{N-2}{N-1}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}-\max_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{p,j}-\frac{1}{N-1}\alpha_{0,j}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq_alpha_jj}}{\geq}\left(\frac{N-2}{2(N-1)}-1\right)\max_{p\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{p,j}-\frac{1}{2(N-1)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq_alpha_ij}}{\geq}\left(\frac{N-2}{2(N-1)}-1\right)\cdot\frac{2}{N}-\frac{1}{2(N-1)}=\frac{-3}{2(N-1)},

(111)

where in the first inequality we also use the fact that
\alpha_{0,j}^{(t,k)}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, since by (
57
) we have
\alpha_{0,j}^{(t,k)}\leq\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}-\frac{N+1}{N-1}\max_{0\leq p\leq S\atop p\notin\{j\}}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{p,j}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:small_gap_2}}{\geq}-\frac{3}{2S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:two_thirds}}{\geq}-\frac{9}{2S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq_alpha_jj}}{\geq}-\frac{9}{S(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_x_jj}}{\geq}-\frac{9}{N-1}\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

(112)

Analogously, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}-\max_{0\leq p\leq S\atop p\neq j}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{p,j}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:small_gap_1}}{\geq}-\frac{1}{SN}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:two_thirds}}{\geq}-\frac{2}{SN}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq_alpha_jj}}{\geq}-\frac{4}{SN}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_y_jj}}{\geq}-\frac{9}{N-1}\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

(113)

Combining the above two inequalities with (
4
), we have the desired bound

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\geq\frac{9}{N-1}\left(\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right).

B.1.6
Proof of Lemma
6

By (
50a
) and (
51a
), we have

\displaystyle\forall j\in[S]:\quad-\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}-\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\geq-\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\geq\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\left(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(114)

Besides, when
n_{-1}^{(k)}=j
, we have
N_{j,j}^{(k-1)}=1
, and

\displaystyle\forall t\leq T_{1},j\in[S]:\quad\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}=\frac{\exp\left(H_{j,j}^{(t)}\right)}{\sum_{0\leq i\leq S}N_{i}^{(k-1)}\exp\left(H_{i,j}^{(t)}\right)}\overset{\eqref{eq:IH1}}{\geq}\frac{\exp\left((1-\frac{9}{N-1})H_{j,j}^{(t)}\right)}{\sum_{0\leq i\leq S}N_{i}^{(k-1)}},

(115)

which indicates

\forall t\leq T_{1}:\quad\frac{1}{N+m}\leq\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\leq\frac{1}{2},\,\,\forall j\in[S],

where we use the fact that
\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}
are equal for any
j\in[S]
due to the symmetry of our training distribution.
By (
114
) we have

\displaystyle-\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}-\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\geq\frac{1}{S(N+m)}\left(\frac{N+m-1}{N+m}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2S(N+m)}.

(116)

(
115
) also implies that

\displaystyle\forall t\leq T_{1}:\quad H_{j,j}^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{1-\frac{9}{N-1}}\log\left((N+1)\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,1)}\right)\leq 3\log\left(\frac{N+1}{2}\right).

(117)

The above two expressions together with (
53
) imply that

\displaystyle\frac{m\eta}{2S(N+m)}T_{1}\leq 3\log\left(\frac{N+1}{2}\right),

which gives the desired result.

B.1.7
Proof of Lemma
7

For notation simplicity, we let
n_{-1}=n_{-1}^{(k)}
.
By (
50b
), we know that

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(k)}_{s(j),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\left(\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}+\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(k)}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]}_{(a)}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{S-3}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{(t,k)}_{i,j}+\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(i),j}\right)\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\right]}_{(b)}.

(118)

By symmetry and the fact that
N_{i}^{(k-1)}\leq 2
for all
i\in[S]
, we have

\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\frac{2\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)}{N-2}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(t,k)}\leq\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}

since
N_{s(j)}^{(k-1)}=1
.
Thus we have

\displaystyle(a)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{S(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4(N-1)}{S(S-1)(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(119)

Similarly, we have

\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}+\alpha_{s(i),j}^{(t,k)}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\frac{3\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)}{N-2}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\leq 2\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

Further, by (
109
) we have when
t\leq T_{1}
,

\displaystyle 1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\geq\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{N}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle(b)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12(S-3)N}{S(S-1)(N-2)^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12N(N-3)}{S(S-1)(N-2)^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12N}{S(S-1)(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right].

(120)

Plugging (
B.1.7
) and (
B.1.7
) into (
B.1.7
), we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16N}{S(S-1)(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_x_jj}}{\leq}-\frac{16N}{(S-1)(N-2)}\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

(121)

Analogously, by (
51b
) we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{i,j}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\left(\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}+\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\right)\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right].

(122)

Note that

\displaystyle\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\cdot\frac{2}{N-1}\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)},

(123)

and when
t\leq T_{1}
,

\displaystyle\left(\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\cdot 4\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\frac{\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)}{N-1}\overset{\eqref{eq:two_thirds}}{\leq}\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\cdot\frac{8}{N-1}\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

(124)

Plugging the above two expressions into (
122
), we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{i,j}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\cdot\frac{10}{N-1}\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{10}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_y_jj}}{\leq}-\frac{10}{S-1}\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

(125)

Combining (
121
) and (
B.1.7
), we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\leq-\frac{16N}{(S-1)(N-2)}\left(\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right)\leq-\frac{19}{S-1}\left(\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right).

(126)

This indicates that for all
i,j\in[S]
,
i\neq j
,

\displaystyle\forall t\leq T_{1}:\quad H_{i,j}^{(t)}\geq-\frac{19}{S-1}H_{j,j}^{(t)}.

B.1.8
Proof of Lemma
8

For notation simplicity, we let
n_{-1}=n_{-1}^{(k)}
. The proof is similar to that of Lemma
7
, except that after iteration
T_{1}
, we have the following tighter bound:

\displaystyle\alpha_{s(j),j}^{(t,k)}\leq\frac{1}{2(N-1)}\overset{\eqref{eq:one_third}}{\leq}\frac{3\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}}{2(N-1)},\quad\text{and}\quad\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\leq\frac{3\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}}{N-1},

(127)

and thus

\displaystyle(a)\leq\frac{2N+1}{S(S-1)(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],

(128)

where (a) is defined in (
B.1.7
).
When
t\leq T_{1}+1
, we also have

\displaystyle\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\leq\frac{2\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)}{N-1}\overset{\eqref{eq:one_third}}{\leq}\frac{6\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)}{N-1},

(129)

where the second inequality also uses the fact that
\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}
increases after
t>T_{1}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle(b)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18(S-3)}{S(S-1)^{2}(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18}{S(S-1)(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j,i\notin\{s(j),n_{1}\}\right],

(130)

where (b) is also defined in (
B.1.7
).

Plugging (
128
) and (
B.1.8
) into (
121
), we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2N+19}{S(S-1)(N-2)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\Big|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_x_jj}}{\leq}-\frac{4}{S-1}\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

(131)

Moreover, when
t\geq T_{1}+1
, (
123
) still holds, and we have

\displaystyle\left(\alpha_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\cdot\frac{4\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}}{(N-1)^{2}}\overset{\eqref{eq:one_third}}{\leq}\mathbbm{1}\left\{N_{i}^{(k-1)}\geq 1\right\}\cdot\frac{12\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}}{(N-1)^{2}}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)},

(132)

where the second inequality also uses the fact that
\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}
increases after
t>T_{1}
.
Plugging (
123
) and (
132
) into (
122
), we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{i,j}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2N+10}{S(S-1)(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{S(S-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}\right)^{2}\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_y_jj}}{\leq}-\frac{4}{S-1}\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}.

(133)

Combining (
B.1.8
) and (
B.1.8
), we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{i,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{i,j}^{(t,k)}\leq-\frac{4}{S-1}\left(\delta x_{j,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right).

This combining with (
63
) indicates that for all
i,j\in[S]
,
i\neq j
,

\displaystyle\forall t\geq T_{1}+1:\quad-\left(H_{i,j}^{(t)}-H_{i,j}^{(T_{1}+1)}\right)\leq\frac{4}{S-1}\left(H_{j,j}^{(t)}-H_{j,j}^{(T_{1}+1)}\right).

B.1.9
Proof of Lemma
9

By our choice of
T_{2}
(see (
65
)), we have

\displaystyle\widecheck{\alpha}^{(T_{2})}\coloneqq\frac{\exp(H_{1,1}^{(T_{2})})}{(N+m-2)\exp(H_{2,1}^{(T_{2})})+\exp(H_{1,1}^{(T_{2})})+1}\leq 1-\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon}{2m}}.

By Induction Hypothesis I and II we know that for all
j\in[S]
and
i\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\setminus\{j\}
:

\displaystyle\forall t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\quad H_{i,j}^{(t)}\leq\frac{9}{N-1}H_{j,j}^{(t)}.

Combining the above two expressions, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(H_{1,1}^{(T_{2})})}{(N+m-1)\exp(\frac{9}{N-1}H_{1,1}^{(T_{2})})+\exp(H_{1,1}^{(T_{2})})}\leq 1-\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon}{2m}},

which gives

\displaystyle H_{1,1}^{(T_{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\frac{9}{N-1}}\log\left((N+m-1)\left(\sqrt{\frac{2m}{\epsilon}}-1\right)\right)\leq 3\log\left((N+m-1)\left(\sqrt{\frac{2m}{\epsilon}}-1\right)\right).

By (
68a
), we have

\displaystyle H_{1,1}^{(T_{2})}-H_{1,1}^{(T_{1})}\geq\frac{\eta\epsilon(T_{2}-T_{1})}{4mS}.

Combining the above two expressions, we have

T_{2}-T_{1}\leq\frac{12mS}{\eta\epsilon}\log\left((N+m-1)\left(\sqrt{\frac{2m}{\epsilon}}-1\right)\right).

B.1.10
Proof of Lemma
10

For notation simplicity, we let
n_{-1}=n_{-1}^{(k)}
. From (
49a
), it gives that

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2S}\sum_{j\in[S]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{l\in[S]\atop l\notin\{n_{1},j,s(j)\}}(\alpha^{(t,k)}_{l,j}+\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(l),j})^{2}+(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j})^{2}+(\alpha^{(t,k)}_{0,j})^{2}\right)\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2S}\sum_{j\in[S]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\max_{0\leq i\leq S,\atop i\neq j}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{i,j}\left(\sum_{0\leq i\leq S,\atop l\notin\{n_{1},j,s(j)\}}\alpha^{(t,k)}_{i,j}\right)+(1-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{j,j}-\alpha^{(t,k)}_{s(j),j})^{2}\right)\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S}\sum_{j\in[S]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\right],

and similarly, (
49b
) gives that

\displaystyle\Delta_{y}^{(t,k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2S}\sum_{j\in[S]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{l\in[S],\atop l\neq j}\left(\alpha_{l,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}+\left(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}\right)\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S}\sum_{j\in[S]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\right].

Thus we have when
t\geq T_{2}+1
:

{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathsf{train}}(\theta^{(t)})=\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left(\Delta_{x}^{(t,k)}+\Delta_{y}^{(t,k)}\right)\leq\frac{2}{S}\sum_{k=1}^{m}\sum_{j\in[S]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(1-\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\right)^{2}\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\right]\leq\epsilon,

where the last inequality follows from our choice of
T_{2}
(see (
65
)).

Since
H_{j,j}^{(t)}
is strictly increasing after iteration
T_{2}
, we have

\forall t\geq T_{2}+1:\quad\frac{\exp(H_{j,j}^{(t)})}{\exp(H_{j,j}^{(t)})+1}\geq\alpha_{j,j}^{(t,k)}\geq 1-\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon}{2m}},

from which we deduce (
76
).

B.2
Proof of Theorem
5

For any non-leaf node
i
, we let
c_{1}(i)
and
c_{2}(i)
denote the left child and right child of node
i
, respectively. Let
\widetilde{c}_{1}(i)=c_{1}(i)
for any
i
that’s not a leaf node, and we define
\widetilde{c}_{1}(g)=\widetilde{c}_{1}(n_{2^{m}})=0
. We also define
\widetilde{p}(i)=p(i)
for any
i
that’s not the root, and
\widetilde{p}(r)=\widetilde{p}(n_{1})=0
.

Throughout the proof, we suppose Assumption
3
and Assumption
5
hold,
N
is a sufficiently large constant,
\eta\lesssim\frac{1}{Nm}
, and
\epsilon\lesssim 1/\mathsf{poly}(N)
is sufficiently small.

Loss simplification and gradient computation.

Letting

x^{(k)}\coloneqq o^{(k)}(1:d_{1}),\quad y^{(k)}\coloneqq o^{(k)}(d_{1}+1:2d_{1}),\quad z^{(k)}\coloneqq o^{(k)}(2d_{1}+1:2d_{1}+d_{2}),

\widehat{x}^{(k)}\coloneqq\widehat{o}^{(k)}(1:d_{1}),\quad\widehat{y}^{(k)}\coloneqq\widehat{o}^{(k)}(d_{1}+1:2d_{1}),\quad\widehat{z}^{(k)}\coloneqq\widehat{o}^{(k)}(2d_{1}+1:2d_{1}+d_{2}),

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}^{(k)}\coloneqq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathcal{T}}\sim P_{\mathsf{train}}}\left\|x^{(k)}-\widehat{x}^{(k)}\right\|_{2}^{2},\;\Delta_{y}^{(k)}\coloneqq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathcal{T}}\sim P_{\mathsf{train}}}\left\|y^{(k)}-\widehat{y}^{(k)}\right\|_{2}^{2},\;\Delta_{z}^{(k)}\coloneqq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathcal{T}}\sim P_{\mathsf{train}}}\left\|z^{(k)}-\widehat{z}^{(k)}\right\|_{2}^{2},

(134)

we can rewrite the loss function as

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathsf{train}}=\sum_{k=1}^{2m}\left(\Delta_{x}^{(k)}+\Delta_{y}^{(k)}+\Delta_{z}^{(k)}\right).

(135)

Recalling
\widetilde{A}\coloneqq(a_{0},a_{1},\ldots,a_{S})\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times(S+1)}
and
\widetilde{S}=(s_{f},s_{b})
, we further define the following matrices:

\begin{split}U_{l}&\coloneqq\widetilde{A}^{\top}B_{l}\widetilde{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{(S+1)\times(S+1)},\quad V_{l}\coloneqq\widetilde{A}^{\top}C_{l}\widetilde{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{(S+1)\times(S+1)},\quad l\in\{1,2\},\\
U_{3}&\coloneqq\widetilde{S}^{\top}B_{3}\widetilde{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2},\quad\quad V_{3}\coloneqq\widetilde{S}^{\top}C_{3}\widetilde{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2},\end{split}

(136)

whose entries are denoted by
U_{l,i,j}
and
V_{l,i,j}
, respectively.
Define the following gradients for all
k\in[2m]
and
l\in\{1,2,3\}
as

\begin{split}\delta x_{l,i,j}^{(k)}&\coloneqq\frac{\partial\Delta_{x}^{(k)}}{\partial U_{l,i,j}},\quad\quad\delta y_{l,i,j}^{(t,k)}\coloneqq\frac{\partial\Delta_{y}^{(k)}}{\partial U_{l,i,j}},\quad\quad\delta z_{l,i,j}^{(k)}\coloneqq\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial V_{l,i,j}},\end{split}

(137)

since
\Delta_{x}^{(k)}
and
\Delta_{y}^{(k)}
depend only on
U_{l}
, and
\Delta_{z}^{(k)}
depends only on
V_{l}
.

We define the following attention weights:

\displaystyle\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{N_{p,q,u}^{(k-1)}\exp\left(a_{p}^{\top}B_{1}a_{j}+a_{q}^{\top}B_{2}a_{j}+s_{u}^{\top}B_{3}s_{v}\right)}{\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime},u^{\prime}}N_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime},u^{\prime}}^{(k-1)}\exp\left(a_{p^{\prime}}^{\top}B_{1}a_{j}+a_{q^{\prime}}^{\top}B_{2}a_{j}+s_{u^{\prime}}^{\top}B_{3}s_{v}\right)},

(138a)

\displaystyle\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{N_{p,q,u}^{(k-1)}\exp\left(a_{p}^{\top}C_{1}a_{j}+a_{q}^{\top}C_{2}a_{j}+s_{u}^{\top}C_{3}s_{v}\right)}{\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime},u^{\prime}}N_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime},u^{\prime}}^{(k-1)}\exp\left(a_{p^{\prime}}^{\top}C_{1}a_{j}+a_{q^{\prime}}^{\top}C_{2}a_{j}+s_{u^{\prime}}^{\top}C_{3}s_{v}\right)},

(138b)

where
u,v\in\{0,1\}
,
p,q,j\in\{0,1,\ldots,S\}
, and
N_{p,q,u}^{(k)}=N_{p,q,u}^{(k)}({\mathcal{T}})
denotes the number of times
(a_{p},a_{q},s_{u})^{\top}
appearing in the columns of
E^{(k-1)}=E^{(k-1)}({\mathcal{T}})
(set
N_{p,q,u}^{(k)}=0
if the combination
(a_{p},a_{q},s_{u})^{\top}
does not exist in
E^{(k-1)}
). Then we have

\forall k\in[2m]:\quad N_{p,q,u}^{(k-1)}\in\{0,1,2\},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{p,q,u}\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}=1,\quad\sum_{p,q,u}\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}=1.

(139)

We simplify the loss function as in the following lemma, whose proof is given in Appendix
B.2.4
.

Lemma 11

(Loss simplification)
.

Suppose Assumption
3
and Assumption
5
hold.
For
\Delta_{x}^{(k)}
, we have

\displaystyle\forall k\in[m]:\quad\Delta_{x}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{-1}^{(k)}=j\}\bigg\{\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(j),j,1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(q),q,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg],

(140a)

\displaystyle k=m+1:\quad\Delta_{x}^{(m+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{1}=j\}\bigg\{\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(q),q,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg],

(140b)

\displaystyle m+2\leq k\leq 2m:\quad\Delta_{x}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{-1}^{(k)}=j\}\bigg\{\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(j),j,1),(j,0)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(q),q,0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,0)}\right)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg].

(140c)

For
\Delta_{y}^{(k)}
, we have

\displaystyle\forall k\in[m]:\;\Delta_{y}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{-1}^{(k)}=j\}\bigg\{\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),s(j),0),(j,1)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(n_{-1},1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(n_{-1},1)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{q\in[S]\atop q\neq p(j)}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg],

(141a)

\displaystyle k=m+1:\;\Delta_{y}^{(m+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{1}=j\}\bigg\{\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(n_{2},c_{1}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(n_{2},c_{2}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(n_{2},j,1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,j,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{q\in[S]\atop q\neq n_{2}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg],

(141b)

\displaystyle m+2\leq k\leq 2m:\;\Delta_{y}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j\in[S]}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{-1}^{(k)}=j,c_{1}(j)=i\}\bigg\{\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{1}(i),0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{2}(i),0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,j,1),(j,0)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,0)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{q\in[S]\atop q\neq i}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,0)}\right)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg].

(141c)

For
\Delta_{z}^{(k)}
, we have

\displaystyle k\in[m]:\;\Delta_{z}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{-1}^{(k)}=j\}\bigg\{\bigg(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg],

(142a)

\displaystyle k=m+1:\;\Delta_{z}^{(m+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{1}=j\}\bigg\{\bigg(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg],

(142b)

\displaystyle m+2\leq k\leq 2m:\;\Delta_{z}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{1}=j\}\bigg\{\bigg(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,0)}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}\bigg].

(142c)

Here,
n_{-1}^{(k)}
denotes the node index of the embedding
x_{-1}^{(k-1)}
.

We also provide a simplified expression of
\delta x_{l,i,j}^{(k)}
,
\delta y_{l,i,j}^{(k)}
,
\delta z_{l,i,j}^{(k)}
in the following lemma, whose proof is given in Appendix
B.2.5
.

Lemma 12

(Gradient expression)
.

We have for all
i\in\{0,\ldots,S\}
,
j\in[S]
,
k\in[2m]
,
\xi\in\{x,y\}
:

\displaystyle\delta\xi_{1,i,j}^{(k)}=\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial U_{1,i,j}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(p,q,u,v)\atop p\neq i}\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}}\bigg(-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}\sum_{s\in[S]\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,s,w),(j,v)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(q,u,v)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,q,u),(j,v)}}\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,q,u),(j,v)}\bigg(1-\sum_{s\in[S]\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,s,w),(j,v)}\bigg),

(143a)

\displaystyle\delta\xi_{2,i,j}^{(k)}=\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial U_{2,i,j}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(p,q,u,v)\atop q\neq i}\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}}\bigg(-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{(r,i,w),(j,v)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(p,u,v)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,i,u),(j,v)}}\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,i,u),(j,v)}\bigg(1-\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\alpha^{(k)}_{(r,i,w),(j,v)}\bigg),

(143b)

and for any
u,v\in\{0,1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta\xi_{3,u,v}^{(k)}=\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial U_{3,u,v}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(p,q,j)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u^{c}),(j,v)}}\bigg(-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u^{c}),(j,v)}\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop s\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(r,s,u^{c}),(j,v)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(p,q,j)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{\xi}^{(k)}}{\partial\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}}\alpha^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}\bigg(1-\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop s\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(r,s,u),(j,v)}\bigg),

(143c)

where

u_{c}=1-u
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\delta z_{1,i,j}^{(k)}=\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial V_{1,i,j}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(p,q,u,v)\atop p\neq i}\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}}\bigg(-\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}\sum_{s\in[S]\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\beta^{(k)}_{(i,s,w),(j,v)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(q,u,v)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial\beta^{(k)}_{(i,q,u),(j,v)}}\beta^{(k)}_{(i,q,u),(j,v)}\bigg(1-\sum_{s\in[S]\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\beta^{(k)}_{(i,s,w),(j,v)}\bigg),

(144a)

\displaystyle\delta z_{2,i,j}^{(k)}=\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial V_{2,i,j}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(p,q,u,v)\atop q\neq i}\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}}\bigg(-\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\beta^{(k)}_{(r,i,w),(j,v)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(p,u,v)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial\beta^{(k)}_{(p,i,u),(j,v)}}\beta^{(k)}_{(p,i,u),(j,v)}\bigg(1-\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop w\in\{0,1\}}\beta^{(k)}_{(r,i,w),(j,v)}\bigg),

(144b)

and for any
u,v\in\{0,1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta z_{3,u,v}^{(k)}=\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial V_{3,u,v}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(p,q,j)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u^{c}),(j,v)}}\bigg(-\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u^{c}),(j,v)}\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop s\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(r,s,u^{c}),(j,v)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(p,q,j)}\frac{\partial\Delta_{z}^{(k)}}{\partial\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}}\beta^{(k)}_{(p,q,u),(j,v)}\bigg(1-\sum_{r\in\{0,\ldots,S\}\atop s\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(r,s,u),(j,v)}\bigg).

(144c)

Define

\displaystyle w_{0}^{(k)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},

(145)

\displaystyle w_{1}^{(k)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\sum_{q\in[S]}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},

(146)

which represent the attention put on the tokens with stage embedding
s_{f}
and
s_{b}
respectively at the
k
-th reasoning step. We have

\displaystyle\forall k\in[2m]:\quad w_{0}^{(k)}+w_{1}^{(k)}=1.

(147)

We also define

\displaystyle p_{0}^{(k)}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\beta^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\beta^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},\quad q_{0}^{(k)}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\beta^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\beta^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},

(148)

for all
i\in\{0,\ldots,S\}
, we define

\displaystyle p_{1,i}^{(k)}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\beta^{(k)}_{(i,\widetilde{c}_{1}(i),0),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\beta^{(k)}_{(i,\widetilde{c}_{1}(i),0),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},\;p_{2,i}^{(k)}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\beta^{(k)}_{(i,c_{2}(i),0),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\beta^{(k)}_{(i,c_{2}(i),0),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},

(149)

and for any
i\in[S]
, we define

\displaystyle p_{i}^{(k)}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(i),i,0),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(i),i,0),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},\;q_{i}^{(k)}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(i),i,1),(n_{-1},1)},&k\in[m+1]\\
\beta^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(i),i,1),(n_{-1},0)},&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases}.

(150)

We further simplify
\delta z_{l}^{(k)}
(
l\in\{1,2,3\}
) in the following lemma, whose proof is given in Appendix
B.2.6
.

Lemma 13
.

We have

\displaystyle\forall j\in[S]:\quad\delta z_{1,0,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{2}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{0}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{1}^{(k)}\left(-\frac{q_{0}^{(k)}}{w_{1}^{(k)}}+\frac{p_{0}^{(k)}}{w_{0}^{(k)}}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],&k\in[m]\\
\frac{2}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{1}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{0}^{(k)}\left(-\frac{p_{0}^{(k)}}{w_{0}^{(k)}}+\frac{q_{0}^{(k)}}{w_{1}^{(k)}}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],&k\in\{m+1,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases};

(151)

\displaystyle\forall i,j\in[S]:\quad\delta z_{1,i,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{2}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{0}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{1}^{(k)}\left(-\frac{q_{p(i)}^{(k)}}{w_{1}^{(k)}}+\frac{p_{1,i}^{(k)}+p_{2,i}^{(k)}}{w_{0}^{(k)}}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],&k\in[m]\\
\frac{2}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{1}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{0}^{(k)}\left(-\frac{p_{1,i}^{(k)}+p_{2,i}^{(k)}}{w_{0}^{(k)}}+\frac{q_{p(i)}^{(k)}}{w_{1}^{(k)}}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],&k\in\{m+1,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases};

(152)

\displaystyle\forall i,j\in[S]:\quad\delta z_{2,i,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{2}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{0}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{1}^{(k)}\left(-\frac{q_{i}^{(k)}}{w_{1}^{(k)}}+\frac{p_{i}^{(k)}}{w_{0}^{(k)}}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],&k\in[m]\\
\frac{2}{S}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{1}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{0}^{(k)}\left(-\frac{p_{i}^{(k)}}{w_{0}^{(k)}}+\frac{q_{i}^{(k)}}{w_{1}^{(k)}}\right)\Big|n_{-1}=j\right],&k\in\{m+1,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases};

(153)

and

\displaystyle\delta z_{3,0,1}^{(k)}=-\delta z_{3,1,1}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{0}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{1}^{(k)}\right],&k\in[m]\\
2\mathbb{E}\left[-\left(w_{1}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{0}^{(k)}\right],&k=m+1\\
0,&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases},

(154)

\displaystyle\delta z_{3,0,0}^{(k)}=-\delta z_{3,1,0}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}0,&k\in[m+1]\\
2\mathbb{E}\left[-\left(w_{1}^{(k)}\right)^{2}w_{0}^{(k)}\right],&k\in\{m+2,\ldots,2m\}\end{cases}.

(155)

We also have

\displaystyle\forall i,j\in\{0,\ldots,S\}:\quad\delta z_{1,i,0}^{(k)}=\delta z_{2,0,j}^{(k)}=0.

(156)

We next give a lemma that simplifies the gradient expressions of
\delta x_{l}^{(k)}
and
\delta y_{l}^{(k)}
, for
l=1,2,3
.

Lemma 14
.

For any
j\in[S]
, we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{1,0,j}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[-\left(1-\alpha^{(1)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\left(1-\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}\right)^{2}\left(1-\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(1-\alpha^{(1)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(1)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}\left(\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j,n_{1}\}}\left(\alpha^{(1)}_{(p(q),q,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(1)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}\left(\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(1)}_{(0,j,1),(j,1)}\right)\bigg|n_{2^{m}}=j\bigg].

(157)

For
k\in\{2,\ldots,m\}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{1,0,j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},1),(j,1)}\right)^{2}\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},1),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(n_{2^{m}}),n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(n_{2^{m}}),n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(n_{2^{m}}),n_{2^{m}},0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(n_{2^{m}}),n_{2^{m}},0),(j,1)}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(j),j,1),(j,1)}\right)\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(j),j,1),(j,1)}\right)\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(1-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(0,j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(c_{1}(j),j,1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(c_{1}(j),j,1),(j,1)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(p(q),q,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(c_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(c_{1}(q),q,1),(j,1)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\Bigg].

(174)

For
k\in\{m+2,\cdots,2m\}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta x_{3,0,1}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\delta x_{3,1,1}^{(k)}=0,

(175)

and

\displaystyle\delta x_{3,0,0}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\delta x_{3,1,0}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[-\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(c_{1}(j),j,1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,0)}\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),q,1),(j,0)}\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(c_{1}(j),j,1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(c_{1}(j),j,1),(j,0)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(q),q,0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(c_{1}(q),q,1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(c_{1}(q),q,1),(j,0)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\Bigg].

(176)

For
k\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{3,0,0}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\delta y_{3,1,0}^{(k)}=0,

(177)

and

\displaystyle\delta y_{3,0,1}^{(k)}=-\delta y_{3,1,1}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[-\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),s(j),0),(j,1)}\right)\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),s(j),0),(j,1)}\right)\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},1),(j,1)}\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{p(j)\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\right)\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 284.52756pt\cdot\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{p(j)\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\Bigg].

(178)

For
k=m+1
, we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{3,0,0}^{(m+1)}

\displaystyle=-\delta y_{3,1,0}^{(m+1)}=0,

(179)

and

\displaystyle\delta y_{3,0,1}^{(m+1)}=-\delta y_{3,1,1}^{(m+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[-\left(1-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},j,1),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},c_{1}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},c_{2}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\cdot\left(\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},c_{1}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},c_{2}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}\right)\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(0,j,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(0,j,0),(j,1)}\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{n_{2}\}}\left(\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\right)\left(\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 284.52756pt\cdot\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(1-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},j,1),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},c_{1}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},c_{2}(n_{2}),0),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(n_{2},j,1),(j,1)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{n_{2}\}}\left(\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\right)\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,1)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(m+1)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,1)}\right)\Bigg|n_{-1}=j\Bigg].

(180)

For
k\in\{m+2,\cdots,2^{m}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta y_{3,0,1}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=-\delta y_{3,1,1}^{(k)}=0,

(181)

and

\displaystyle\delta y_{3,0,0}^{(k)}=-\delta y_{3,1,0}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\sum_{i\in[S]}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbbm{1}\{n_{2^{k-m}}=i\}\bigg\{-\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{1}(i),0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{2}(i),0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,j,1),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\cdot\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{1}(i),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{2}(i),0),(j,0)}\right)\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,0)}\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{i\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\cdot\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,0)}\right)\left(1-\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{1}(i),0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,c_{2}(i),0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,j,1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,j,1),(j,0)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(j,0)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(j,0)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{i\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{1}(q),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,c_{2}(q),0),(j,0)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,0)}\right)\alpha^{(k)}_{(q,p(q),1),(j,0)}\left(\sum_{q\in[S]}\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{p}(q),q,0),(j,0)}\right)\Bigg\}\bigg|n_{-1}=j\Bigg].

(182)

Lemma
14
can be verified by straightforward calculation, whose proof is thus omitted for conciseness.

B.2.1
Proof outline

Let the iteration-varying versions of (
136
) be

\displaystyle U_{1}^{(t)}\coloneqq\widetilde{A}^{\top}B_{1}^{(t)}A\in\mathbb{R}^{(S+1)\times S},\,\ U_{2}^{(t)}\coloneqq A^{\top}\widetilde{B}_{2}^{(t)}A\in\mathbb{R}^{S\times S},\,\ U_{3}^{(t)}\coloneqq\widetilde{S}^{\top}\widetilde{B}_{3}^{(t)}\widetilde{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2},

(183a)

\displaystyle V_{1}^{(t)}\coloneqq\widetilde{A}^{\top}C_{1}^{(t)}A\in\mathbb{R}^{(S+1)\times 2},\,\ V_{2}^{(t)}\coloneqq A^{\top}C_{2}^{(t)}A\in\mathbb{R}^{S\times 2},\,\ V_{3}^{(t)}\coloneqq\widetilde{S}^{\top}C_{3}^{(t)}\widetilde{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2},

(183b)

and the index starts from
0
for
U^{(t)}_{l}
and
V^{(t)}_{l}
when
l=1,2
(i.e.,
U_{l,0,j}=a_{0}^{\top}B_{l}^{(t)}a_{j}
,
V_{l,0,j}=a_{0}^{\top}C_{l}^{(t)}a_{j}
).
Then we have for all
l\in[3]
:

\displaystyle\forall i\in\{0,\ldots,S\},j\in[S]:\quad U_{l,i,j}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle=U_{l,i,j}^{(t)}-\eta\sum_{k=1}^{2m}\left(\delta x_{l,i,j}^{(t,k)}+\delta y_{l,i,j}^{(t,k)}\right),

(184a)

\displaystyle V_{l,i,j}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle=V_{l,i,j}^{(t)}-\eta\sum_{k=1}^{2m}\delta z_{l,i,j}^{(t,k)}.

(184b)

Therefore, it is possible to analyze the dynamics of
U_{l}^{(t)}
and
V_{l}^{(t)}
separately.

Similar as the backward case, by symmetry of the training distribution, we have for any
t\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
i,j\in[S]
,
i\neq j
,
U_{l,i,j}^{(t)}
(
l=1,2
) are equal to each other,
U_{l,j,j}^{(t)}
(
l=1,2
) are equal to each other, and
U_{l,0,j}^{(t)}
are equal to each other. Note that
a_{0}
only appears in
X^{(k)}
but never appears in
Y^{(k)}
, thus
U_{1,j,0}^{(t)}=U_{2,j,0}^{(t)}=U_{2,0,j}^{(t)}=0
for all
t\in{\mathbb{N}}
. The same holds for
V_{l,i,j}^{(t)}
(
l=1,2
).
Therefore, we could define the following pattern:

\displaystyle U_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&-a^{(t)}\mu_{1}^{(t)}&-a^{(t)}\mu_{1}^{(t)}&\cdots&-a^{(t)}\mu_{1}^{(t)}\\
0&\nu_{1}^{(t)}&\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}\\
0&\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}&\nu_{1}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
0&\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}&\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{1}^{(t)}\end{pmatrix},\quad U_{2}^{(t)}=\begin{pmatrix}0&0&0&\cdots&0\\
0&\mu_{1}^{(t)}&\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}\\
0&\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}&\mu_{1}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
0&\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}&\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}&\cdots&\mu_{1}^{(t)}\end{pmatrix},

(185)

and

\displaystyle V_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&\mu_{2}^{(t)}&\mu_{2}^{(t)}&\cdots&\mu_{2}^{(t)}\\
0&\nu_{2}^{(t)}&\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}\\
0&\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}&\nu_{2}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
0&\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}&\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{2}^{(t)}\end{pmatrix},\quad V_{2}^{(t)}=\begin{pmatrix}0&0&0&\cdots&0\\
0&b^{(t)}\mu_{2}^{(t)}&\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}\\
0&\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}&b^{(t)}\mu_{2}^{(t)}&\cdots&\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
0&\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}&\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}&\cdots&b^{(t)}\mu_{2}^{(t)}\end{pmatrix},

(186)

for some
a^{(t)},b^{(t)},\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(t)},\nu_{1}^{(t)},\nu_{2}^{(t)},\nu_{1,1}^{(t)},\nu_{1,2}^{(t)},\nu_{2,1}^{(t)},\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}\in{\mathbb{R}}
. We set
a^{(0)}=b^{(0)}=1
.

Regarding
U_{3}^{(t)}
and
V_{3}^{(t)}
, by Lemma
13
and Lemma
14
, it follows for
\xi\in\{x,y,z\}

\displaystyle\forall t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\quad

\displaystyle\delta\xi_{3,0,1}^{(t)}=-\delta\xi_{3,1,1}^{(t)},\quad\text{and}\quad\delta\xi_{3,0,0}^{(t)}=-\delta\xi_{3,1,0}^{(t)}.

Thus by our initialization (
U_{3},V_{3}=0
), we have

\displaystyle\forall t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\quad

\displaystyle U_{3,0,1}^{(t)}=-U_{3,1,1}^{(0)},\quad\text{and}\quad U_{3,0,0}^{(t)}=-U_{3,1,0}^{(0)},

\displaystyle V_{3,0,1}^{(t)}=-V_{3,1,1}^{(0)},\quad\text{and}\quad V_{3,0,0}^{(t)}=-V_{3,1,0}^{(0)}.

Therefore, we could define

\displaystyle U_{3}^{(t)}=\mu_{1}^{(t)}\begin{pmatrix}-b_{1}^{(t)}&b_{2}^{(t)}\\
b_{1}^{(t)}&-b_{2}^{(t)}\end{pmatrix},\quad V_{3}^{(t)}=\mu_{2}^{(t)}\begin{pmatrix}c_{1}^{(t)}&-c_{2}^{(t)}\\
-c_{1}^{(t)}&c_{2}^{(t)}\end{pmatrix}

(187)

for some
b_{1}^{(t)},b_{2}^{(t)},c_{1}^{(t)},c_{2}^{(t)}\in{\mathbb{R}}
, where
\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(t)}
is defined in (
185
) and (
186
).

B.2.2
Training dynamics of
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}

Training dynamics of Phase I.1-C.

Define

\displaystyle T_{1}^{C}\coloneqq\max\left\{t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\exp(\mu_{2}^{(t)})\leq\sqrt{2N}\right\}.

(188)

We show that the following key relations hold during Phase I.1-C.

Lemma 15
.

Assume the learning rate satisfies
\eta\lesssim\frac{1}{Nm}
. When
t\in[T_{1}^{C}]
, we have
\mu_{2}^{(t)}
monotonically increases, and

\displaystyle\mu_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{t\eta}{SN^{3/2}},

(189a)

\displaystyle b^{(t)}\mu_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1\pm{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log N}{N}\right)\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},

(189b)

\displaystyle|\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log N}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},\quad|\nu_{2}^{(t)}|={\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log N}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},\quad|\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}|={\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log N}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},

(189c)

\displaystyle c_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq 1/2.

(189d)

The proof of Lemma
15
is given in Appendix
B.2.7
.
By (
189a
) and (
188
), we know that

\displaystyle T_{1}^{C}\lesssim\frac{SN^{3/2}\log N}{\eta}.

(190)

Training dynamics of Phase I.2-C.

We let

\displaystyle T_{2}^{C}\coloneqq\max\left\{t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\exp(\mu_{2}^{(t)})\leq N\right\}.

(191)

We will show the following relations hold during Phase I.2-C.

Lemma 16
.

Assume the learning rate satisfies
\eta\lesssim\frac{1}{Nm}
. When
t\in\{T_{1}^{C},\cdots,T_{2}^{C}\}
, we have
\mu_{2}^{(t)}
monotonically increases, and

\displaystyle\mu_{2}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(T_{1}^{C})}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\eta(t-T_{1}^{C})}{SN},

(192a)

\displaystyle b^{(t)}\mu_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1\pm{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log^{2}N}{N}\right)\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},

(192b)

\displaystyle|\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log N}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},\quad|\nu_{2}^{(t)}|={\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log N}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},\quad|\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}|={\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\log N}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},

(192c)

\displaystyle c_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq 1/4.

(192d)

At the end of Phase I.2-C, we have
c_{2}^{(T_{2}^{C})}\in[0.25,0.26]
, and
c_{1}^{(T_{2}^{C})}\geq 0.48
.

The proof of Lemma
16
is given in Appendix
B.2.8
.
By (
192a
), we know that

\displaystyle T_{2}^{C}-T_{1}^{C}\lesssim\frac{SN\log N}{\eta}.

(193)

Training dynamics of Phase II-C.

We set

\displaystyle T_{3}^{C}\coloneqq\max\left\{t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\frac{N}{\exp\left(0.47\mu_{2}^{(t)}\right)+N}\geq\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon}{6m}}\right\}.

(194)

We show the following relations hold during Phase II-C.

Lemma 17
.

Assume
\epsilon_{0}>0
is small enough obeying
\epsilon_{0}\lesssim 1/\mathsf{poly}(N)
and
\eta\lesssim\frac{1}{Nm}
. When
t\in\{T_{2}^{C},\cdots,T_{3}^{C}\}
, for any
\epsilon\in(0,\epsilon_{0}]
, we have
\mu_{2}^{(t)}
monotonically increases, and

\displaystyle\mu_{2}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(T_{2}^{C})}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\left(t-T_{2}^{C}\right)\eta}{S}\left(\frac{\epsilon}{m}\right)^{3/2},

(195a)

\displaystyle b^{(t)}\mu_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1\pm\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\right)\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},

(195b)

\displaystyle|\nu_{2,1}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},\quad|\nu_{2}^{(t)}|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},\quad|\nu_{2,2}^{(t)}|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)},

(195c)

\displaystyle c_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\in[0.24,0.26],\quad c_{1}^{(t+1)}\geq c_{2}^{(t+1)}-\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\right).

(195d)

The proof of Lemma
17
is given in Appendix
B.2.9
.
By (
194
) and (
195a
), we have

\displaystyle T_{3}^{C}-T_{2}^{C}\lesssim\frac{S}{\eta}\left(\frac{m}{\epsilon}\right)^{3/2}\log\left(\frac{N}{\epsilon}\right).

(196)

It’s easy to see from the proof of Lemma
17
that
\mu_{2}^{(t)}
keeps increasing after
T_{3}^{C}
and (
195b
), (
195c
) and (
195d
) hold for all
t\geq T_{3}^{C}
. We give the following lemma to mark the convergence of
\sum_{k=1}^{2m}\Delta_{z}^{(k)}
, whose proof is given in Appendix
B.2.10
.

Lemma 18
.

After
t\geq T_{3}^{C}+1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{2m}\Delta_{z}^{(k)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\epsilon}{3}.

(197)

B.2.3
Training dynamics of
B_{1},B_{2},B_{3}

Training dynamics of Phase I.1-B.

We define

\displaystyle T_{1}^{B}\coloneqq\max\left\{t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\exp\left(\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)\leq N\right\},

(198)

and call the period
t\leq T_{1}^{B}
as Phase I.1-B. The following lemma gives the training dynamics of
U_{1},U_{2},U_{3}
in Phase I.1-B.

Lemma 19
.

When
t\in\{0,1,\cdots,T_{1}^{B}\}
, we have
\mu_{1}^{(t)},b_{1}^{(t)}\mu_{1}^{(t)}
monotonically increase,
b_{2}^{(t)}\mu_{1}^{(t)}
first decreases and then increases, and

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\eta t}{NS},

(199a)

\displaystyle b_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2},\,\,\text{and}\,\,\exp\left((1-2b_{2}^{(t)})\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)\lesssim\frac{N}{m}\quad\text{when }b_{2}^{(t)}\leq 0,

(199b)

\displaystyle\nu_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right),\left|\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}\right|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right),\left|\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}\right|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right),

(199c)

\displaystyle\exp\left((1-a^{(t)})\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim N.

(199d)

The proof of Lemma
19
is given in Appendix
B.2.12
.
By (
199b
) we know that
b_{2}^{(T_{1}^{B})}\geq 0
, and by (
199d
) we know that
a^{(T_{1}^{B})}\geq 0
. Further, by (
199a
) we know that

\displaystyle T_{1}^{B}\lesssim\frac{NS\log N}{\eta}.

(200)

Training dynamics of Phase I.2-B.

Let

\displaystyle T_{2}^{B}\coloneqq\max\left\{t\geq T_{1}^{B}:\exp\left((1-2b_{2}^{(t)})\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)\geq C_{0}N\right\},

(201)

where
C_{0}
is a sufficiently large constant.
Without loss of generality, we assume
T_{2}^{B}>T_{1}^{B}
.
We call the period
t\in\{T_{1}^{B},T_{1}^{B}+2,\cdots,T_{2}^{B}\}
as Phase I.2-B. The following lemma formally describes the training dynamics of
U_{1},U_{2},U_{3}
in Phase I.2-B, whose proof is given in Appendix
B.2.13
.

Lemma 20
.

When
t\in\{T_{1}^{B},T_{1}^{B}+2,\cdots,T_{2}^{B}\}
, we have
\mu_{1}^{(t)},b_{1}^{(t)}\mu_{1}^{(t)}
monotonically increase, and

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{(t)}-\mu_{1}^{(T_{1}^{B})}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\eta\left(t-T_{1}^{B}\right)}{NS},

(202a)

\displaystyle\exp\left((1-a^{(t)})\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim N,

(202b)

\displaystyle 0\leq b_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2},

(202c)

\displaystyle\nu_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right),\left|\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}\right|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right),\left|\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}\right|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right).

(202d)

At the end of Phase I.2-B, we have

\displaystyle\exp\left((1-a^{(T_{2}^{B})})\mu_{1}^{(T_{2}^{B})}\right)\asymp N,

(203a)

\displaystyle b_{2}^{(T_{2}^{B})}=\left(1/4\pm\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{N})\right)a^{(T_{2}^{B})}.

(203b)

By (
202a
) and (
203b
) we know that

\displaystyle T_{2}^{B}-T_{1}^{B}\lesssim\frac{NS\log N}{\eta}.

(204)

Training dynamics of Phase II-B.

We define

\displaystyle T_{3}^{B}\coloneqq\max\left\{t\in{\mathbb{N}}:\frac{N+2m-1}{\exp\left(0.46\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)+N+2m-1}\geq\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon}{6m}}\right\},

(205)

and call the period
t\in\{T_{2}^{B},T_{2}^{B}+1,\cdots,T_{3}^{B}\}
as Phase II-B.
We’ll show that at the start of the next phase (i.e., shortly after
T_{2}^{B}
),
\exp\left(\left(1-a^{(t)}\right)\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)
increases to, and stays at
{\mathcal{O}}(N)
, indicating that when training long enough,
a^{(t)}
will approach
1
,
b_{2}^{(t)}
will approach and stay at
0.25
, and
b_{1}^{(t)}
is larger than
b_{2}^{(t)}
.
The training dynamics of
U_{1},U_{2},U_{3}
in Phase II-B is formally given in the following lemma, whose proof is given in Appendix
B.2.14
.

Lemma 21
.

When
t\in\{T_{2}^{B},T_{2}^{B}+1,\cdots,T_{3}^{B}\}
, we have
\mu_{1}^{(t)},b_{1}^{(t)}\mu_{1}^{(t)}
monotonically increase, and

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{(t)}-\mu_{1}^{(T_{2}^{B})}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\left(t-T_{2}^{C}\right)\eta}{S}\left(\frac{\epsilon}{m}\right)^{3/2},

(206a)

\displaystyle\exp\left((1-a^{(t)})\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\asymp N,

(206b)

\displaystyle b_{2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\in\left[0.24,0.26\right]a^{(t)},

(206c)

\displaystyle\nu_{1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right),\left|\nu_{1,1}^{(t)}\right|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right),\left|\nu_{1,2}^{(t)}\right|=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t)}}{N}\right).

(206d)

At the end of Phase II-B, we have

\displaystyle a^{(T_{3}^{B})}

\displaystyle\in[0.99,1),

(207a)

\displaystyle b_{1}^{(T_{3}^{B})}

\displaystyle\geq b_{2}^{(T_{3}^{B})}+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\right).

(207b)

By (
206a
) we know that

\displaystyle T_{3}^{B}-T_{2}^{B}\lesssim\frac{S}{\eta}\left(\frac{m}{\epsilon}\right)^{3/2}\log\left(\frac{N}{\epsilon}\right).

(208)

After Phase II-B.

It’s easy to see from our proof that after Phase II-B,
\mu_{1}^{(t)}
keeps increasing (but slowly), (
206d
) and (
207
) of Lemma
21
still hold.
After Phase II-B, the loss reaches
\epsilon
as indicated by the following lemma, whose proof is given in Appendix
B.2.15
.

Lemma 22
.

After
t\geq T_{3}^{B}+1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{2m}\left(\Delta_{x}^{(t,k)}+\Delta_{y}^{(t,k)}\right)\leq\frac{2\epsilon}{3}.

(209)

B.2.4
Proof of Lemma
11

Note that when
k\in[m]
we have
n_{-1}^{(k)}=n_{2^{m-k+1}}
, and hence by (
9
),
when
k\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\|a_{n_{-1}}-\sum_{i=1}^{2^{m}-1}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,2i,0),(n_{-1},1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(i,2i+1,0),(n_{-1},1)}\right)a_{2i+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{1},0),(n_{-1},1)}a_{n_{1}}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(0,n_{2^{m}},1),(n_{-1},1)}a_{n_{2^{m}}}-\sum_{l=m-k+2}^{m}\alpha^{(k)}_{(n_{2^{l}},n_{2^{l-1}},1),(n_{-1},1)}a_{n_{2^{l-1}}}\Bigg\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{S}\mathbb{E}\Biggl\|\mathbbm{1}\{n_{-1}=j\}\Bigg\{\left(1-\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(j),j,0),(j,1)}-\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(j),j,0),(j,1)}\right)a_{j}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt-\sum_{q\in[S]\setminus\{j\}}\left(\alpha^{(k)}_{(p(q),j,0),(j,1)}+\alpha^{(k)}_{(\widetilde{c}_{1}(q),j,0),(j,1)}\right)a_{q}\Bigg\}\Biggr\|^{2}_{2}.

(210)

By Assumption
